A Geography of Human Rights Abuses
page=40 block=866648070 (x=87 y=1) width=152.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima-Bold wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
1083
page=40 block=399769606 (x=305 y=1) width=20.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=1 wordint.max=18
geographers can use when approaching human  rights  issues.  Over  the 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=25) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
twentieth and twenty-first centuries geographers have fashioned and revised 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=37) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
a series of organizational and methodological “traditions.”181 In addition the 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=48) width=327.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
subject matter of geography has been divided into categories: systematic 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=61) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
(concentrating on specific subjects such as political geography, economic 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=73) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
geography,  urban  geography,  and  environmental  geography),  regional 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=85) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
geography, and spatial techniques (including quantitative and qualitative 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=97) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
analysis, computer cartography, GIS, and remote sensing). Contemporary 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=109) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
geographers conduct  their research within  a  multitude of  philosophical 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=121) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
and epistemological orientations including positivism, humanism, Marxism 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=133) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
(radical and critical), postmodernism, feminism, applied, post-structuralism, 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=145) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
and post-colonialism.182 Furthermore, contemporary geographers have much 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=156) width=327.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
more sophisticated means of accessing, mapping, and analyzing data than 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=169) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
previously dreamed. This means that geographers focusing on human rights 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=181) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
issues have multiple ways of approaching and reporting their findings. The 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=193) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
challenge for human rights researchers is to discover how this variety of ap-
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=205) width=324.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
proaches can be incorporated in their work. For geographers the challenge 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=217) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
is two-fold: to make their work more accessible to the human rights com-
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=229) width=324.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
munity by publishing more broadly and to incorporate the research of the 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=241) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
human rights community into their own work. This dual strategy approach 
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=253) width=327.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
has proven useful in Asian and Jewish Studies.183
page=40 block=21436422 (x=1 y=264) width=212.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=Optima wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
181.
